## Triage Notes

- Critical findings are the first repair candidates: especially console logging in clinical chat paths that includes patient identifiers or message content.
- The `Dead imports - imported nowhere` group is a static import graph result, not an automatic deletion list. Tests, scripts, migrations, app entrypoints, generated files, framework-discovered files, and config files often have no inbound static imports by design.
- The exact requested root TypeScript command is currently broken as a diagnostic because there is no root `tsconfig.json`; frontend and backend explicit project checks were clean.
- The ESLint output indicates repo-level ESLint configuration issues for TypeScript parsing and runtime globals in addition to genuine unused values.
